title: Jspreadsheet Demo sales
keywords: Jspreadsheet, JavaScript data grid, spreadsheet controls, interactive data grid, web-based applications, lightweight, responsive controls, agnostic platform, Angular, React, Vue, spreadsheet-like plugin, documentation, examples, license
description: Interactive sales dashboard with JavaScript spreadsheet UI. Visualise targets, revenue, and conversion metrics in real time.

# Sales Dashboard

This demo presents a sales performance dashboard powered by a JavaScript spreadsheet component. It combines visual indicators, charts, and editable cells to track revenue, targets, conversion rates, and sales KPIs—ideal for web applications that require interactive analytics.

<br>

<div class="demo-code">

```html
<html>
<script src="https://jspreadsheet.com/v11/jspreadsheet.js"></script>
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://jspreadsheet.com/v11/jspreadsheet.css" type="text/css" />
<script src="https://jsuites.net/v5/jsuites.js"></script>
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://jsuites.net/v5/jsuites.css" type="text/css" />
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css?family=Material+Icons" />
<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@jspreadsheet/formula-pro/dist/index.min.js"></script>
<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js@4.4.0/dist/chart.umd.js"></script>
<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@jspreadsheet/formula-charts@4.0.0/dist/index.min.js"></script>

<div id="spreadsheet"></div>

<script>

// Set your JSS license key (The following key only works for one day)
jspreadsheet.setLicense('###license###');

jspreadsheet.setExtensions({ formula });
``` 

```jsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Spreadsheet, Worksheet, jspreadsheet } from '@jspreadsheet/react';
import charts from '@jspreadsheet/charts';
import formula from '@jspreadsheet/formula-pro';

import '@lemonadejs/studio';
import '@lemonadejs/studio/dist/style.css';
import 'jsuites/dist/jsuites.css';
import 'jspreadsheet/dist/jspreadsheet.css';
import '@jspreadsheet/formula-charts';
import '@jspreadsheet/charts/dist/style.css';
import './App.css';

// Set your JSS license key (The following key only works for one day)
const license = '###license###';

export default function App() {
  const spreadsheetRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    jspreadsheet.setLicense(license);
    jspreadsheet.setExtensions({ formula });
```

```angularjs
import { Component, ElementRef, ViewChild } from '@angular/core';
import jspreadsheet from 'jspreadsheet';
import formula from '@jspreadsheet/formula-pro';
import '@jspreadsheet/formula-charts';

// Set your JSS license key (The following key only works for one day)
jspreadsheet.setLicense('###license###');

jspreadsheet.setExtensions({ formula });

@Component({
  standalone: true,
  selector: 'app-root',
  template: `<div #spreadsheet></div>`,
})
export class AppComponent {
  @ViewChild('spreadsheet', { static: true }) spreadsheet!: ElementRef;

  ngAfterViewInit() {
    jspreadsheet(this.spreadsheet.nativeElement, {
      style: [
        'font-size: 13px;font-family: Bahnschrift;color: #000000;text-align: center;vertical-align: middle;',
        'background-color: #C00000;font-size: 13px;font-family: Bahnschrift;color: #FFFFFF;text-align: center;vertical-align: middle;',
        'background-color: #000000;font-size: 13px;font-weight: bold;font-family: Bahnschrift;color: #FFFFFF;text-align: center;vertical-align: middle;border-left: 1px solid #D9D9D9;border-top: 1px solid #D9D9D9;',
        'background-color: #C00000;font-size: 13px;font-weight: bold;font-family: Bahnschrift;color: #FFFFFF;text-align: center;vertical-align: middle;border-left: 1px solid #D9D9D9;border-top: 1px solid #D9D9D9;',
        'background-color: #7030A0;font-size: 13px;font-weight: bold;font-family: Bahnschrift;color: #FFFFFF;text-align: center;vertical-align: middle;border-left: 1px solid #D9D9D9;border-top: 1px solid #D9D9D9;',
```
